## Playful Bathroom Mirror Selfie Prompt (Curvy Focus)

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
"subject": "Young woman with fair skin and voluminous, shoulder-length wavy black hair. She is wearing a tight, red short-sleeved t-shirt that is fitted to her torso and dark grey, tight athletic shorts ('booty shorts') that accentuate the glutes. On her left wrist, she wears a thin gold bangle bracelet. Her fingernails are long, manicured with white french tips. She is holding a blue smartphone with an orange camera module and a pink 'pop-it' fidget toy case attachment.",
"pose": "Mirror selfie stance with a strong torso twist. The subject stands with her back and hips angled towards the mirror, twisting her upper body deeply to the left to face the reflection. Her hips are pushed back and resting against the white bathroom vanity counter, emphasizing a curvy lower body silhouette. Her left arm is bent, holding the phone up to face level to take the photo. Her right arm is raised, with her hand resting high on the white doorframe for support. Her head is tilted slightly to the right, and she is making a playful pouting 'kissy face' expression.",
"environment": "A domestic bathroom interior. The foreground features a white countertop. To the immediate left, a dark grey textured bathrobe hangs on a hook against a tiled wall. The subject is framed by a white doorframe. Through the open door behind her (visible in the mirror reflection), a bedroom is seen with cool blue ambient lighting and a white cherry blossom branch decal on the wall. The floor appears to be light-colored tile.",
"camera": "Mirror selfie perspective. The shot is taken from the reflection in a bathroom mirror. The camera angle is slightly high, looking down at the hips and level with the face. The phone itself is visible in the frame, obscuring part of the subject's hand. The framing captures from the mid-thighs up to above the head, centered on the subject's reflection.",
"lighting": "Mixed lighting. The foreground (subject and bathroom) is illuminated by bright, neutral-white overhead bathroom vanity lighting, creating soft highlights on the skin and hair. The background (bedroom) is bathed in a distinct cool blue ambient light, creating a color contrast between the warm/neutral foreground and the cool background.",
"mood_and_expression": "Playful, confident, and flirty. The subject's expression is a deliberate pout (duck face), and the pose is calculated to highlight her physique. The vibe is casual, typical of a social media 'fit check' or lifestyle update.",
"style_and_realism": "High-fidelity smartphone photography. Realistic skin textures, natural fabric folds on the tight clothing, and accurate capturing of the mirror reflection physics. Not stylized; represents a candid real-world snapshot aesthetic.",
"colors_and_tone": "Vibrant contrast. The bright red of the shirt stands out against the neutral whites and greys of the bathroom and shorts. The blue phone case and blue background lighting add cool tone accents. Skin
```

## Ultra-Realistic Surreal Conceptual Portrait of a Baby in a Gigantic Flower Field

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic surreal conceptual portrait of a cute baby standing in the middle of a gigantic flower field, where the flowers are impossibly tall like trees and their petals are wider than umbrellas. The baby wears a soft pastel outfit (tiny cardigan and romper) with a cozy hat, chubby cheeks, bright curious eyes, and an innocent dreamy expression, looking upward in awe.

The massive flowers are hyper-detailed with realistic petal veins, dew droplets, and glowing pollen floating through the air like golden dust. Some petals bend gently toward the baby as if protecting them, forming a magical canopy overhead. Soft sunbeams stream down through the towering stems, creating volumetric light rays and a misty fairytale haze.

Cinematic wide-angle composition with shallow depth of field, ultra-sharp focus on the baby’s face and nearby petals, distant flowers fading into soft bokeh. Warm pastel color grading, natural baby skin texture, editorial fantasy photography style, surreal realism, 8K quality, vertical composition, magical wholesome atmosphere.
```

## Cinematic Outdoor Portrait in Streetwear at Golden Hour

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Cinematic Outdoor Portrait Captured In Warm Natural Daylight, With The Subject Seated Casually On The Edge Of A Textured Concrete Structure. The Framing Is Slightly Low-angled And Mid-distance, Placing The Subject Against A Vast, Uninterrupted Sky In Soft Cyan-blue (#8ec9e8), Creating A Clean, Minimalist Backdrop. Lighting Is Golden-hour Warm (-6200k), Casting Soft Highlights Across The Face And Clothing, While Producing A Long, Crisp Shadow On The Wall Below, The Pose Is Relaxed And Editorial: Hands Resting At The Sides, Legs Extended With One Foot Slightly Raised, And A Calm, Introspective Facial Expression Directed Toward The Camera. Wardrobe Features Oversized Casual Streetwear-muted Rust-colored Hoodie, Wide Beige Corduroy Pants, Striped Socks, And Clean White Sneakers-styled In Matte Textures That Absorb The Sunlight Softly. The Concrete Surface Adds Subtle Grain And Visual Weight, Contrasting With The Airy Sky. Lens Simulation Approximates A 50mm Full-frame At F/2.8, Providing Moderate Depth Of Field With Sharp Detail On The Subject And Gentle Natural Falloff Into The Sky. The Color Palette Blends Warm Earth Tones With Bright Atmospheric Blues, Creating A Serene, Youthful, And Editorial Mood. Editorial, Modern, High-detail, Cinematic Composition, Ultra-realistic Textures, Professional Fashion Photography Style
```

## Editorial Portrait in Museum with Classical Statue

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photoreal_museum_classical_statue_editorial_portrait",
      "version": "v1.0_MUSEUM_STATUE_TOUCH_CREAM_CORSET_DRESS_SOFT_SUN_NO_TEXT_EN",
      "priority": "highest",
      "language": "en"
    },

    "references": {
      "reference_image_style": {
        "source": "UPLOAD_REFERENCE_IMAGE (REQUIRED)",
        "purpose": "STYLE_COMPOSITION_LIGHTING_ANCHOR",
        "strict_lock": true,
        "preserve_composition": true,
        "preserve_lighting": true,
        "preserve_palette": true,
        "preserve_vibe": true
      },
      "reference_image_face": {
        "source": "UPLOAD_REFERENCE_IMAGE (OPTIONAL)",
        "purpose": "FACE_IDENTITY_LOCK",
        "strict_lock": true,
        "face_similarity_priority": "MAX",
        "no_identity_blending": true,
        "no_beautify": true,
        "no_age_shift": true,
        "preserve_skin_texture": true,
        "preserve_facial_proportions": true,
        "preserve_moles_freckles_scars": true
      }
    },

    "global_constraints": {
      "rating": "PG-13",
      "no_nudity": true,
      "no_explicit_sexual_content": true,
      "no_logos": true,
      "no_watermark": true,
      "no_readable_text": true
    },

    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "9:16",
      "orientation": "portrait",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "cinematic_photoreal_editorial",
      "sharpness": "crisp_but_natural",
      "film_grain": "subtle_35mm",
      "color_grade": "warm_creamy_beige_soft_sun",
      "dynamic_range": "natural_not_hdr",
      "skin_rendering": "real_texture_no_retouch"
    },

    "creative_prompt": {
      "scene_summary": "Create a photoreal editorial portrait in a classical museum/gallery space with pale stone walls and soft midday sunlight. Composition: vertical 9:16.\
\
Foreground left: a large white marble classical statue (seated figure) with realistic stone texture, chips, and patina. The statue is modest and museum-appropriate.\
\
Foreground right: one adult woman standing in profile facing left, gently touching the statue’s cheek/chin with her right hand (a delicate, respectful gesture). Her expression is calm and contemplative, looking toward the statue.\
\
Wardrobe & styling: cream/beige corset-style top with visible lace-up ribbon detailing and decorative trim; paired with an ivory/white long skirt. Accessories: small gold earrings, thin bracelet, a small shoulder bag with a chain strap. Hair: dark brown, half-up with soft waves, neat and glossy. Makeup: natural, warm, minimal.\
\
Background: a framed abstract artwork on the wall (NO readable text, NO logos), softly out of focus. Lighting: natural sun beam with gentle shadows and realistic highlights on marble and skin. Camera feel: premium candid editorial, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture, no over-retouching.\
\
Keep everything elegant, quie"
```

```
A close-up studio portrait of me, masculine features, textured messy hair neatly styled.
I wear a black tailored blazer with sharp structure and clean lines, layered over a black high-neck turtleneck, embodying a minimalist and contemporary fashion style.
The man is wearing bold translucent orange acetate sunglasses, rectangular frame with slightly rounded edges, glossy finish, amber/orange tinted lenses, and fashion-forward and statement eyewear. Color concept: selective color photography - monochrome black and white image with only the sunglasses in vibrant orange color, creating strong contrast and visual focus.
Expression: calm, confident, serious gaze, looking straight into the camera.
Lighting: soft studio lighting from the front with subtle side shadows, even skin tones, greath detai,l facial texture, and cinematic contrast. Camera settings:
- Lens: 85mm portrait lens
- Aperture: f/2.0
- ISO: 100
- Shutter speed: 1/125
- High resolution, ultra-sharp focus on face Style: editorial fashion portrait, luxury menswear, realistic skin texture, non-Al look, professional studio photography.
```

On her feet are red suede sneakers with white three stripes (Adidas style). In one hand she holds a classic glass bottle of Coca-Cola. Next to her, on the bench, is a small red shoulder bag (Prada style).

```
In the background is a white painted brick wall. To the left, a large potted plant (Dracaena) with tall, green leaves is visible. The ground is gray concrete. The lighting is bright, natural daylight, casting harsh, realistic shadows on the scene. The colors are vibrant, and the style is urban/street fashion.
```

## Black-and-White Surreal Editorial Portrait

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the woman's exact face without altering it. Black-and-white surreal editorial portrait: clean profile. Hand at chin in an elegant gesture. She wears a black textured voluminous top with a fur/feather effect, her hair is loose, and round earrings catch the light. Subtle horizontal motion blur runs across the top of the frame. Soft, diffused, high-contrast light: the face is sharp, the rest of the frame is cast into shadow. The background is minimalist, white with gray transitions. Close-up angle, shallow depth of field, glossy fashion style, slight grain. Negative: anatomy distortions, extra hands, incorrect lighting, lack of motion blur, cartoonish look, low contrast, overexposure, artifacts.
```

```
An antique music box open on a vanity table, with the {argument name="subject" default="SUBJECT"} rising from the tiny spinning dancer platform in a transformation from miniature mechanism to full-scale manifestation. The figure begins as painted porcelain at the feet, transitions through jointed doll anatomy, into breathing dimensional presence at the face and hands. {argument name="key features" default="KEY FEATURES"} achieve impossible life while the base remains fixed to the rotation gear. The music box melody is visible as golden spiraling notation wrapping the ascending form. Clockwork visible inside the box, gears still turning, powering the miracle. Music box chime tones translated to warm light, intimate boudoir atmosphere, 8K, mechanical romance.
```

## Variable Fisheye POV Wildlife Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
DIRECTIVE:
Generate a raw, hyper-realistic POV wildlife photograph shot on an action camera (GoPro style).

SUBJECT ({VAR_SUBJECT}):
A {argument name="subject" default="pristine white Arctic Fox"} captured at intimate, point-blank range.
Pose: The {VAR_SUBJECT} is extremely close to the lens, inspecting it curiously. This creates the signature wide-angle facial distortion: a prominent {argument name="facial feature" default="cute wet black nose, massive fluffy ears, and curious whiskers"}, massive head proportions, and intense eye contact.
The Eyes: {argument name="eyes description" default="Large, striking golden-brown eyes"}. They must feature a sharp reflection of the {VAR_REFLECTION}, conveying wild intelligence.
Texture: The skin or fur details are incredibly high-definition, featuring {VAR_TEXTURE_DETAILS}.

ENVIRONMENT ({VAR_BIOME}):
The backdrop is {VAR_LOCATION_DESC}.
Terrain: Sharp contrast between {VAR_TERRAIN_DETAILS}.
Atmosphere: {VAR_ATMOSPHERE}. The air feels distinct and immersive. The background stretches out endlessly due to the wide focal length.

TECHNICAL SPECS (THE GOPRO LOOK):
Lens: 16mm Ultra-Wide Angle (Fisheye effect).
Focus: Macro Wide. The focus is locked sharply on the subject's eyes and nose, while the distant horizon fades into a soft, atmospheric blur (Depth of Field).
Lighting: {VAR_LIGHTING}. Soft, diffuse, shadowless ambient light that enhances the subject against the terrain.
Post-Processing: RAW aesthetic. No filters, slight digital noise (grain), natural color grading, high dynamic range to capture details without blowing out the highlights.

VARIABLES (Artic Fox example, tweak the prompt to your needs)

{VAR_SUBJECT} = pristine white Arctic Fox

{VAR_FACIAL_FEATURE} = cute wet black nose, massive fluffy ears, and curious whiskers

{VAR_EYES_DESC} = Large, striking golden-brown eyes

{VAR_REFLECTION} = endless snowy horizon and overcast sky

{VAR_TEXTURE_DETAILS} = incredibly dense, multi-layered white fur with actual snowflakes trapped in the coat

{VAR_BIOME} = FROZEN ARCTIC TUNDRA

{VAR_LOCATION_DESC} = a vast, snow-covered wilderness expanse

{VAR_TERRAIN_DETAILS} = fresh powder snow, jagged dark volcanic rocks protruding, and patches of ice

{VAR_ATMOSPHERE} = Freezing, crisp, and misty

{VAR_LIGHTING} = Blue Hour (Nordic twilight; soft, cold, diffuse light before sunrise)
```

## 4-Panel Collage Portrait with Flash Lighting

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "image_request": {
    "format": "4-panel collage",
    "style": "Realistic portrait",
    "perspective": "High-angle shot, close to face",
    "constraints": "Face must be 100% identical across all panels",
    "subject": {
      "appearance": "Beautiful young woman with fair, smooth, flawless skin that looks healthy and naturally glowing. Shiny brown hair styled in a loose bun (messy bun). Soft, gentle makeup with flushed pink lips.",
      "accessories": [
        "Thick, large, transparent clear-frame glasses with a pink tone (clear frame 1.2)",
        "Small silver butterfly necklace"
      ],
      "clothing": "{argument name="clothing item" default="Pink T-shirt"}",
      "gaze": "Directly at the camera"
    },
    "lighting_and_tone": {
      "lighting_type": "Direct flash with a soft, diffused glow effect",
      "lighting_characteristics": "Flash light spreads evenly, highlights slightly overexposed, soft reflective highlights on skin",
      "color_palette": "Creamy white / creamy mauve",
      "skin_glow": "Slightly pinkish glow"
    },
    "panels": {
      "panel_1": "{argument name="panel 1 pose" default="Sweet laughing pose"}",
      "panel_2": "Peace sign pose",
      "panel_3": "Metal hand sign pose",
      "panel_4": "Cute pose"
    },
    "full_prompt_text": "Create a realistic portrait image in a 4-panel collage with a high-angle shot perspective (the face must be 100% identical — do not change or modify it). Depict a beautiful young woman with fair, smooth, flawless skin that looks healthy and naturally glowing. She has shiny brown hair styled in a loose bun (messy bun). Her makeup uses soft, gentle tones, with flushed pink lips. The camera angle is close to the face. She is wearing thick, large, transparent clear-frame glasses with a pink tone (clear frame 1.2). She wears a pink T-shirt and a small silver butterfly necklace. Flash lighting illuminates the face and skin, creating soft reflective highlights and giving the skin a slightly pinkish glow. The overall color tone of the image is creamy white / creamy mauve. She looks directly at the camera. Use direct flash with a soft, diffused glow effect, where the flash light spreads evenly and the highlights are slightly overexposed. Panel 1: Sweet laughing pose. Panel 2: Peace sign pose. Panel 3: Metal hand sign pose. Panel 4: Cute pose."
  }
}
```

## Antarctica Penguin Adventure (2 Variations)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt Image 1:- A wide-angle selfie photograph in Antarctica showing a man sitting on the snow, surrounded by a large colony of Emperor penguins. The man has short dark hair, a full beard, and is wearing red-lensed metal-frame sunglasses, a dark blue unbuttoned denim jacket over a white crew-neck t-shirt, dark blue cargo pants, and a brown canvas backpack. He is leaning slightly back, holding an orange smartphone in his right hand to take the selfie, with his left hand near the backpack strap. Around him, dozens of adult Emperor penguins are standing and walking on the snow, some close to the camera and others stretching into the distance. The background features massive towering glaciers and ice mountains with white cliffs and blue ice formations, deep snow with visible footprints, and a clear bright blue sky. Bright natural daylight creates crisp shadows, with sharp focus on the man, penguins, and the vast icy landscape, captured from a slightly low perspective that emphasizes scale and isolation.

Prompt Image 2:- A highly detailed, cinematic wide-angle photograph in Antarctica: a handsome young man with short dark neatly styled hair, a full trimmed beard, and a confident expression is kneeling on one knee in the deep snow, gently shaking hands / holding flipper with a large adult Emperor penguin standing upright in front of him. The man is wearing red-tinted metal-frame aviator sunglasses, a dark blue unbuttoned denim jacket over a white crew-neck t-shirt, dark blue cargo pants, brown canvas backpack with straps over both shoulders, white Nike sneakers, and a wristwatch. He is looking at the penguin with a slight smile, his right hand clasping the penguin's flipper. The Emperor penguin is life-size, black head with yellow-orange neck patches, looking directly at the man. Surrounding them is a vast icy Antarctic landscape with massive towering glaciers, snow-covered mountains, blue ice formations, and distant icebergs under a clear bright blue sky. Crisp natural daylight, sharp shadows, high detail on textures of snow, denim fabric, penguin feathers, and facial features. Slightly low camera angle to emphasize scale and adventure, photorealistic style, sharp focus throughout, vibrant yet natural colors & heartwarming composition.
```

## Mirror Selfie Aesthetic with Detailed Physics and Identity Locking

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Header] (3:4)
Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro sensor simulation, diffused indoor ambient lighting, zero bokeh, SHARP focus from the foreground carpet textures to the background closet tracks, f/5.6 aperture for structural depth, subtle digital grain.

[Subject & Identity Locking]
Identity: Absolute preservation of {argument name="reference image" default="Image A"}'s specific facial geometry, focusing on the lip volume, cheekbone structure, and soft-glam aesthetic.
Hair Physics: Voluminous dark chocolate textured waves, layered with frizz-free definition. The hair should cascade naturally over the shoulders and down the back, showing high-definition strand-level detail.
Bio-Fidelity: Detailed skin physics including micro-pores, satin-finish hydration, and visible fine vellus hair along the jawline. Precise anatomical rendering of the hands, including realistic finger-joint articulation and a polished white manicure.
Expression: Mirror-selfie aesthetic; the face is partially obscured by the technical device, capturing a focused gaze directed at the reflection.
[Outfit & Physicality]
Technical Description: High-performance athletic loungewear. An oversized black technical-cotton sweatshirt with a cropped silhouette.
Lower Geometry: Technical micro-performance-wear shorts in matte charcoal grey, featuring a high-cut V-shape waistline and seamless construction.
Textile Physics: High-tensile material-to-skin surface compression. The grey shorts must exhibit realistic physical tension lines across the gluteal proportions and proximal limb geometry. The black sweatshirt shows heavy fabric folding and realistic volume around the torso and arms.
Footwear: Low-cut white cotton crew socks with a soft knit texture.
Physicality: Confident self-assured posture. The body is positioned in a "torsion S-curve," kneeling on the floor with the torso rotated 180 degrees to face the mirror, highlighting the waist-to-hip ratio and symmetrical lower-body silhouette.
[Pose & Composition]
Geometry: Low-angle mirror-reflection framing. The subject is kneeling on a high-pile neutral-toned carpet.
Framing: Vertical orientation capturing the full lower-body geometry and the upper-body rotation.
Reflection Physics: Accurate rendering of the phone-to-hand contact point in the reflection, with the black smartphone held in a technical grip.
[Environment & Lighting]
Atmosphere: Minimalist modern bedroom setting.
Interior Details: Large white sliding closet doors with vertical metallic tracks; a secondary arched mirror is visible in the reflection. Neutral white walls and a light-beige textured carpet.
Lighting: Soft, top-down diffused lighting (3500K). The light source is positioned to emphasize the anatomical bust volume and the muscle definition in the legs without creating harsh shadows.
Background Integrity: All background elements, including the closet doors and wall seams, must remain in sharp focus and perfectly grid-aligned.
[Failure Prevention Rule]
```

## Cinematic Winter Photo Shoot Sequence (3 Frames)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Vertical cinematic photo session, 9:16 format.
Use the face and appearance of the person from the attached image without modification. To preserve individual facial features, proportions, skin texture and natural facial expressions as much as possible. Create a photorealistic sequence of three shots, all shot in the same style, like a professional winter photo shoot. Scene: A winter city park in the evening, during blue hour. Snow-covered trees surround, snow gently falling, warm streetlights in the background, bokeh. 
Frame 1: A man sits on a wooden bench, wrapped in a warm coat and scarf, reading a book. Snow slowly settles on hair and clothes. The depth of field is medium, creating an atmosphere of calm and comfort.
Frame 2: Profile portrait, camera closer. Small snowflakes are visible in the air, realistic texture of hair, skin, fabric.
The background is softly blurred, the light from the lanterns creates warm spots of light.
The man leans back on the bench, eyes closed, a slight expression of peace.
Frame 3: Closed left-handed expression. Breathing is slightly noticeable in the cold air. Snow continues to fall, a feeling of peace and quiet.
Photorealism, natural color correction, cinematic lighting, shallow depth of field, realistic light and snow physics.
```

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-fidelity, wide-angle interior shot captures a surreal, mixed-media composition within a modern living room. It features a man resembling the face in the reference photo—using an uploaded face as a reference—wearing a bright yellow hoodie and black pants, sitting centrally on a plush light gray sofa. The scene seamlessly blends photorealistic 3D environments with cel-shaded 2D anime and cartoon characters interacting directly with the physical space.
On the right side of the subject on the sofa, {argument name="character 1" default="Nobita"} sits casually waving his hand, while on the left side, {argument name="character 2" default="Doraemon"} leans casually on a pillow. Behind the sofa, two framed posters hang on the white wall—one featuring Son Goku and Vegeta, and the other featuring Trunks in a dynamic anime style. In the foreground, {argument name="character 3" default="Shinchan"} lies relaxed on a textured gray carpet near a plate of dorayaki, while a chibi version of Son Goku stands triumphantly on a cream-colored knit pouf. On the left side of the room, Vegeta stands tall on a grooved wooden side table in a confident pose, while a miniature version of Trunks is near the wooden coffee table in the center, as if observing the scene with a curious expression.
Soft natural light streams in from the left side through sheer curtains, creating subtle volumetric lighting that accentuates the texture of the blanket, the wood grain of the tiered table, and the leaves of the snake plant in the corner of the room. The entire scene is rendered in 8K resolution with sharp focus, vivid colors, and a dreamy, cinematic, and playful aesthetic blending between the real world and the anime world, with no AI visible.
```

## SZA SOS Album Cover Recreation

> Sinematik, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A square-format album cover scene featuring the person in the reference image as the sole subject. The subject's facial features, skin tone, expression, and hairstyle are identical to the given facial reference; the face remains unchanged.
The subject sits in a relaxed three-quarter profile at the very edge of a white spring-loaded diving board that extends from the right edge of the frame into a wide, open ocean. There is no land or horizon visible; only layered, textured, undulating waves fill the entire background.
The subject is positioned in the right third of the frame, adhering to the rule of thirds. The camera is at eye level, in a three-quarter profile facing left, with the chin slightly raised, legs swinging freely, and hands resting loosely on the knees. The diving board serves as a clean, almost horizontal directional line.
Clothing: {argument name="clothing description" default="oversized white and blue athletic jersey, white shorts, brown boots"}.
The color palette is dominated by cool ocean blues: a deep indigo base, Prussian blue midtones, turquoise-cyan highlights at the wave crests, slate blue shadows, and almost black troughs with subtle silvery glints.
Lighting is provided by soft, diffused sunset light with a slight fall. Overall, a cool, blue hour vibe. Add in the fine-film grain, slight halos, subtle vignetting, slight matte curve, and restrained contrast.
Shot with a cinematic 35mm film look using 135mm telephoto equivalent, f/4, 1/500s, ISO 400. The medium-deep depth of field keeps the subject and board in focus while ensuring the wave texture remains visible.
Minimal typography space is left at the top for album text.
```

```
A young man standing knee deep in fresh snow, arms open wide and slightly raised, head tilted back toward the sky, expressing calm, freedom, and gratitude. The setting is a winter forest landscape with tall pine trees heavily covered in thick snow, stretching across the background. The sky is clear and bright blue with no clouds, sunlight is strong and crisp, casting soft natural shadows on the snow. The overall mood is peaceful, powerful, and raw, blending human presence with nature.
Photorealistic style, ultra high detail, sharp focus, natural daylight, wide angle shot, eye level perspective, cinematic composition, high contrast between dark clothing and white snow, cold winter color tones, realistic textures, no blur, no motion, no stylization.
```

## Mixed-Media Anime and Reality Living Room Scene Prompt

```
A high-fidelity, wide-angle interior shot captures a surreal, mixed-media composition within a modern living room. It features a man resembling the face in the reference photo—using an uploaded face as a reference—wearing a bright yellow hoodie and black pants, sitting centrally on a plush light gray sofa. The scene seamlessly blends photorealistic 3D environments with cel-shaded 2D anime and cartoon characters interacting directly with the physical space.

On the right side of the subject on the sofa, Nobita sits casually waving his hand, while on the left side, Doraemon leans casually on a pillow. Behind the sofa, two framed posters hang on the white wall—one featuring Son Goku and Vegeta, and the other featuring Trunks in a dynamic anime style. In the foreground, Shinchan lies relaxed on a textured gray carpet near a plate of dorayaki, while a chibi version of Son Goku stands triumphantly on a cream-colored knit pouf. On the left side of the room, Vegeta stands tall on a grooved wooden side table in a confident pose, while a miniature version of Trunks is near the wooden coffee table in the center, as if observing the scene with a curious expression.

Soft natural light streams in from the left side through sheer curtains, creating subtle volumetric lighting that accentuates the texture of the blanket, the wood grain of the tiered table, and the leaves of the snake plant in the corner of the room. The entire scene is rendered in 8K resolution with sharp focus, vivid colors, and a dreamy, cinematic, and playful aesthetic blending between the real world and the anime world, with no AI visible.
```

She faces the camera directly with a peek-a-boo effect, her face obscured by loose, messy strands of long dark brown hair falling vertically across the left side of the frame and over the bridge of her nose.
```
One visible almond-shaped brown-hazel eye in sharp focus, piercing and expressive, with a soft catchlight in the pupil and naturally groomed eyebrow. Tiny, simple gold nose stud on the left nostril. Soft muted terracotta-red lipstick, lips gently closed in a subtle half-smile.
Warm, diffused golden-hour lighting with cinematic side lighting, illuminating the eye and cheek while the hair-covered areas fall into soft shadow. Rich warm color palette with browns, warm skin tones, and soft reds. Natural skin texture visible, pores present, slightly grainy cinematic texture (not plastic or overly smooth).
Shot at eye level using an 85mm portrait lens, shallow depth of field, f/1.8, sharp focus on the eye and lashes, emotional, intimate, romantic, mysterious mood, ultra-realistic photography style --ar 2:3 --v 6.0
>NEGATIVE PROMPT
full body, wide shot, forehead fully visible, blue or cold lighting, harsh shadows, overexposed highlights, cartoon, illustration, anime, painting, CGI, plastic skin, overly smooth skin, perfect symmetry, unrealistic beauty standards, distorted face, extra eyes, extra nose, bad anatomy, low resolution, blur, noise artifacts, watermark, text.
```

```
1. Photorealistic, close-up selfie, first-person perspective.
A young woman taking a mirror selfie in a Japanese restaurant, with a wooden wall background featuring a hanging handwritten menu sign.
She is wearing an off-shoulder cream-colored embroidered top, a prominent green bangle, holding an orange iPhone, slightly licking her upper lip and looking at the camera.
Lighting is warm indoor ambient light, with a soft pink glow on the face, and a slight beauty filter soft focus effect. Colors are dominated by warm orange, cream white, and deep forest green, paired with wood tones and dark brown. Emphasize the texture of the top fabric, the wood grain of the wall, and the texture of the phone. The scene atmosphere is warm and private, like a real izakaya selfie. High definition, rich details.

2. Realistic photography, mirror selfie, vertical composition, medium shot. A young woman taking a mirror selfie in a gray tiled bathroom, wearing a textured white bandeau top and white skirt. The core of the image is the strong lens flare and highlight caused by the phone camera flash, brightly illuminating the person and the phone reflection in the mirror, creating high contrast with the dim bathroom environment (including a toilet and dark door). The mirror clearly reflects the person's long hair, focused expression, and the posture of holding the phone. Colors are dominated by white, dark gray, and black, supplemented by warm beige and light gray. Features slight graininess typical of flash in low light. Emphasizes the flash effect and mirror composition of a real phone photo. High definition, rich details.
```

## Cinematic Winter Scene Prompt of Couple Throwing Snow

> Sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A dynamic winter scene in a snowy forest, featuring a young couple playfully throwing show at each other on a wide forest path. The camera is positioned low and slightly angled to the right, facing the couple, capturing motion and spontaneity. In the foreground, a young woman in a long, {argument name="coat color" default="light brown"} teddy coat, white knit bat, white wide-leg pants, warm mittens, and beige boots reacts as a cloud of snow hits her, her posture animated and lively, one arm slightly raised, showing a mix of surprise and joy on her face. In the background, a young man in blue jeans, brown boots, light sweater with beige and white stripes, and a white beanie is mid-throw, his figure slightly blurred to emphasize motion. Both are surrounded by tall, dark pine trees dusted with snow, creating a cozy, cinematic atmosphere. The ground is completely covered with fresh, untouched snow. The overall mood is playful, youthful, and joyful, with an energetic winter vibe. Lighting is soft, ambient daylight, diffused through the forest canopy. The photo is captured with a shallow depth of field (f/2.8), focusing on the woman in the foreground while slightly blurring the background to highlight movement and atmosphere. Colors are natural and muted, with warm beige and brown tones contrasting against cold whites and forest greens. The composition emphasizes activity, emotional connection, and visual storytelling, ideal for high quality editorial or lifestyle winter photography.
```

In the palm of his hand stands a tiny, 3-inch tall miniature version of the exact same man with the same face, but wearing a black t-shirt and tan chinos. The tiny man is looking up at his giant self with a terrified, defensive posture. High-resolution textures showing skin pores, individual beard hairs, and fabric fibers. The background is a warm, out-of-focus domestic interior with soft bokeh from kitchen lights. 8k resolution, photorealistic, shot on 85mm lens.", 'aspect_ratio': '1:1'}"

## Nostalgic Candid Holiday Scene with Motion Blur

> Minimal, retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A candid indoor holiday scene photographed in a warm, nostalgic vintage style. A cozy living room decorated for Christmas, with a fully lit Christmas tree in the background adorned with glowing string lights, ornaments, and a tree topper. Wrapped presents sit beneath the tree, adding soft points of warm light. The room feels intimate and lived-in, with framed photos on the wall and people seated casually on couches in the background, slightly out of focus. In the foreground, a young beautiful attractive blond adult woman is captured mid-movement, dancing naturally. She is smiling while looking slightly downward, joyful and relaxed. She wears a green knit sweater with a subtle festive pattern, tucked into a high-waisted red skirt. A slim belt cinches the waist. She has a red headband in her hair, which is medium-length, brown, and softly styled with natural volume. Her makeup is minimal and natural, emphasizing a warm smile and expressive eyes. Her posture shows motion--one leg bent, hips angled, arms loose--giving the image a spontaneous, authentic feel. The camera angle is slightly low and close, creating a dynamic perspective and emphasizing movement. Lighting is warm and soft, typical of indoor incandescent bulbs and Christmas lights. The image has visible film grain, slight blur from motion, muted contrast, and a faded color palette reminiscent of analog film photography from the late 1990s or early 2000s. Subtle vignetting and texture overlay give the image a scanned photo look. Photorealistic but nostalgic, documentary-style family snapshot, imperfect and charming. No studio lighting, no posed feeling--pure candid holiday moment.
```

## 1980s underground nerd techno party flash photo prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
DIRECTIVE: A photorealistic, candid flash photo from a 1980s underground nerd techno party.

SUBJECT/CHARACTER:
  CRITICAL: Use attached image/video as strict reference for the man's face and mullet hair.
  ACCESSORY: He is wearing white or neon plastic shutter shade sunglasses (horizontal slats).
  EXPRESSION: Mouth slightly open, mid-dance move, looking intense.

SCENE & LAYOUT:
  Environment: A dark, smoke-filled basement disco with cheap laser lights.
  CROWD (Background): A diverse group of "uncool" 80s geeks. Overweight guys with suspenders, skinny girls with frizzy hair and braces, people in homemade "Tron" or "Star Wars" costumes made of foil.
  Action: Everyone is dancing awkwardly to techno music.

TECHNICAL SPECS:
  Camera: 35mm point-and-shoot camera with direct on-camera flash.
  Style: Vintage candid snapshot, high contrast, slight motion blur on the background dancers.
  Vibe: Geek culture, "Freaks and Geeks," authentic 80s awkwardness.
```

## High-Contrast Flash Portrait in Dimly Lit Restaurant Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
•.° - ₊˚ʚ ɞ˚₊ - °.•

Create an image of a young woman. Do not change my face

Pose/Expression:
- She is sitting on a deep red leather banquette that curves around the table. She is leaning forward slightly toward the camera. 
- Both elbows are resting on the table. Her hands are brought up to her head, with her palms gently cradling her temples and her fingers tucked into her hair, creating a soft, contemplative frame for her face. Looking directly to camera lens with a neutral, soft expression.
- Her hips face to the right, with her upper body slightly turned back toward the camera. Crossed-legs at the edge of the table, creating a compact pose. Her lower legs and feet are partly out of frame, but appear tucked back or angled toward the floor.

Outfit/Accessories:
- She is wearing a white halter-neck mini dress. The dress has a high mock-neck collar with a small keyhole cutout at the chest. It features a sleeveless, open-back high-neck design that highlights the shoulders. Made from lightweight, slightly sheer chiffon or organza, it has a bubble or tiered hem that creates a soft, airy shape against the fitted neckline.
- A classic gold-tone wristwatch with a metallic link bracelet, worn on the left wrist.
- Soft beige coffin shape glossy nails

Hair:
- Chest-length black-colored hair with subtle waves. Styled with curtain bangs that framing her face

Makeup:
- She wears soft glam makeup with a natural, luminous finish. Neutral champagne eyeshadow with a subtle shimmer, softly blended brown crease, and thin eyeliner. Long, wispy lashes. Lightly defined brows. Soft peach or rose blush. Nude-pink glossy lips. Elegant, effortless night-time beauty suitable for a dim-lit restaurant setting.

Setting/Background:
- A dimly lit restaurant setting, captured with a blend of elegant and candid aesthetics.
- Behind her is a plain but luxurious looking white wall (on right) and on left side is an ornate display cabinet with glass doors and internal lighting. It houses various glassware (wine and water glasses)
- A dark wood circular table is visible, set with a dark ceramic plate, polished silver cutlery, and a folded patterned table napkin.

Lighting:
- There is a soft glow from direct camera flash hitting her face and shoulder.
- The subject is illuminated by a strong direct on-camera flash. Creating strong contrast and deep shadows in the background. No ambient light, naturally dim atmosphere.

Angle & Framing:
- The shot is taken from a slightly high angle, looking down at the subject.
- It is a medium-full shot. The frame captures her from the head down to her mid-thigh, including a significant portion of the table
```

He is wearing a red and black flannel shirt over a grey t-shirt, baggy blue jeans, and black high-top sneakers. Low-angle perspective, urban aesthetic, soft natural lighting, shallow depth of field, high detail, 4k resolution.
```
​Key Elements Included:
​Lighting: Specifically mentions the "golden hour" and the "warm orange street lamp" to capture that specific mood.
​Composition: Notes the "low-angle perspective" which gives the image its grounded, urban feel.
​Subject Details: Describes the clothing (flannel, baggy jeans) and the specific pose (eyes closed, sitting on the board).
​Environment: Highlights the "weathered brick" and "faded graffiti" to ensure the background texture is correct.
```

## Ethereal Close-Up Portrait Prompt

> Minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
In a vertical composition defined by a 9:16 aspect ratio, the perspective gazes downward upon a scene of intimate proximity and striking visual harmony. The subject is a breathtaking, stunning young woman, appearing in the dewy freshness of her early twenties, supermodel face, whose upward gaze anchors the frame with mesmerizing elegance. Her skin radiates a porcelain fairness, smooth and luminous, contrasting starkly with the blonde, long spaghetti waves of hair that frame her face and the fringe that rests lightly upon her brow. Her features possess a flawless symmetry akin to high-fashion portraiture, characterized by high cheekbones and a delicate jawline. Her eyes are a piercing, translucent blue, profound in depth and accentuated by sharp, winged eyeliner and long lashes, while her big natural lips are full, red-hued, and relaxed in a soft pout.
One of her hand adorned with a simple diamond ring on the ring finger and red nails, gently cups her chin and jaw, tilting her face upward to meet the lens. She appears slender and lithe, her physique draped in a white minidress that exposes her shoulders, arms and deep necklace. The background is a muted, out-of-focus surface of speckled grey concrete, ensuring that the viewer's attention remains fixed solely on the ethereal beauty and captivating expression of the subject. Super close up.
```

## Bossy Gangster with Cheetah Portrait Prompt

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
GENERATE A POWERFUL AND STYLISH IMAGE OF THE UPLOADED MAN, 100 PERCENT FACE RESERVED, RADIATING BOSSY GANGSTER VIBES. HE IS SEATED CONFIDENTLY ON A LUXURIOUS VELVET SOFA, LEANING BACK IN A RELAXED YET COMMANDING POSE. A CHEETAH, ADORNED WITH HEAVY GOLD CHAINS, SITS ACROSS HIS LAP, ITS SLEEK BODY DRAPED NATURALLY OVER HIM.

THE MAN'S HEAD IS TURNED SLIGHTLY DOWNWARD, GAZING AT THE CHEETAH WITH A COOL, COMPOSED EXPRESSION, SUNGLASSES ENHANCING HIS AURA OF DOMINANCE AND STYLE. ONE HAND RESTS CASUALLY ON THE CHEETAH'S BACK WHILE THE OTHER RESTS ALONG THE ARM OF THE SOFA, SHOWING BOTH CONTROL AND EASE.

HE IS DRESSED IN HIGH-FASHION DARK ATTIRE - EITHER A PINSTRIPE SUIT OR AN OPEN-COLLARED SILK SHIRT LAYERED WITH BOLD GOLD JEWELRY. THE CHEETAH HAS A FIERCE, OPEN-MOUTH EXPRESSION, MIRRORING THE MAN'S AURA OF RAW POWER.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Cinematic Neon Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyper realistic cinematic portrait of a young man, natural soft skin with visible texture, subtle pores and fine facial detail. He has a calm but intense expression, a deep focused gaze looking slightly past the camera. Short dark hair, slightly tousled, with soft strands catching the light along the edges. Light stubble or clean shaved look, sharp jawline softly defined by shadow.
He is standing close to a window or glass surface at night, with {argument name="neon colors" default="cherry red and crimson"} neon lights reflecting across his face and surroundings. Strong side lighting creates dramatic contrast, with highlights on the cheekbones, nose, and brow, while deep shadows fall across one side of his face, forming abstract light patterns.
The background is softly blurred with red, wine red, and dark magenta tones, neon signage bokeh, urban night atmosphere. Subtle moisture or reflections on the glass add depth and realism.
Cinematic color grading, moody editorial feel, shallow depth of field, sharp focus on the eyes, glossy skin highlights, studio level detail, high dynamic range, realistic lighting, natural color transitions. High end cinema camera look, 85mm lens feel, f1.8 aperture, professional fashion editorial style, 4:5 image ratio. 
--ar 2:3 --v 6.1 --style raw
```

## Nostalgic Winter Street Fashion Editorial Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photograph taken during a light snowfall of a young adult woman with soft, medium-length, wavy blonde hair, slightly tousled by the wind, a face with natural makeup without heavy make-up, softly lit and partially turned to the side, a slender figure, and an elegant pose, stepping out of an old olive green car. She is wearing a cozy light gray knit midi-length sheath dress with a knee-length slit, over an oversized gray herringbone wool coat, cinched at the waist with a wide brown leather belt-a classic, minimalist street style. Accessories: A small horse-shaped jewelry brooch adorned with ruby stones is pinned to the coat, black leather gloves, a large soft black leather tote bag, and a chunky gold ring on one hand worn over the glove. A natural cedar branch, a sprig with red berries, and the edge of a wrapped Christmas present are visible from the open bag. The scene takes place on a snowy winter city street, with blurred buildings in muted tones creating a cinematic European atmosphere. The textures of the soft woolen yarn of the dress, the textured wool coat, the smooth leather of the gloves and bag, and the metal parts of the car create a sense of the coming New Year. The architecture is decorated in the minimalist style of German cities. The lighting is soft natural daylight with subtle shadows under a slightly overcast sky, creating a diffuse, cinematic glow. The mood is cozy, elegant, cinematic, slightly nostalgic, sophisticated, yet relaxed. The camera is positioned at waist level, looking up as if the viewer is standing at an open car door, with a medium shot capturing the upper body and part of the legs, focusing on the outfit and pose an the viewer exits the car. The style is analog film, with high grain and an editorial street fashion aesthetic, shot on Kodak Portla 400.
```

```
A hyper-realistic, 8k, low-angle shot using a wide lens, looking up at a massive concrete graffiti wall in a derelict train yard. The subject, uploaded face as reference, appears painted as part of a giant, photorealistic street art mural covering the entire wall, but their upper body and head seem to be breaking out of the 2D wall into 3D reality. The "painted" portion of them wears a colorful spray-paint stained hoodie and cap. Their real, 3D face looks down with a rebellious smirk. The surrounding wall is covered in layers of vibrant tags and murals. In the foreground are real rusted train tracks and discarded spray cans. The scene is lit by harsh, direct sunlight creating strong shadows, highlighting the texture of peeling paint and concrete
```

```
Ultra-realistic candid Instagram Story–style photo, as if casually captured on an iPhone 15, vertical orientation 9:16. The image feels spontaneous, unposed, and imperfect — like a quick handheld snap shared directly to Instagram Stories.
Subject:
A young woman sitting at an outdoor Mediterranean-style beachside café, captured in a genuine mid-action moment while eating {argument name="food" default="pasta"}. She is twirling fresh tagliatelle or spaghetti on a fork and bringing it toward her lips.The expression is natural and candid — lips slightly pursed, relaxed face, subtle focus on the food, casual elegance without posing.
Her hair is naturally styled, softly framing the face with realistic texture and slight movement from the sea breeze (no idealized or “perfect” styling). No headwear.
She wears yellow oval sunglasses resting on top of her head, delicate gold rings, and white frangipani-shaped earrings.
Her outfit is a fitted, textured {argument name="outfit color" default="blue"} summer blouse, modestly styled, with soft bell sleeves and visible fabric weave and folds.
Short nails with soft yellow polish, slightly worn at the edges.
Environment:
Sunny beachside café with wooden tables and chairs, cream-toned walls, and a white pergola roof. Uneven striped sunlight passes through the pergola, creating natural light-and-shadow patterns across her face, hair, clothing, and table.
Table details:
A small metal pan with pasta, a casually placed fork and spoon on a white napkin, and a glass of orange aperitif with ice, slight condensation on the glass.
Lighting & Camera Feel:
Natural daylight only, iPhone Smart HDR, handheld feel.
Slight background motion blur, minor framing imperfections, subtle digital noise.
True-to-life colors, no cinematic lighting, no stylization.
Skin & Face:
Realistic skin texture with visible pores, micro-imperfections, natural shine.
Light sun-kissed blush, hydrated natural red lips, defined eyelashes without heavy makeup.
Overall Look:
Authentic travel IG Story aesthetic, smartphone portrait mode but imperfect, no heavy editing, no filters — looks like a real moment captured by chance.
```

## Raw High-Contrast Mirror Selfie (iPhone Ultra-Wide)

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast [Candid Mirror Documentation] captured with an iPhone 17 Pro using the 0.5x ultra-wide-angle lens. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. Every focal plane remains SHARP and in focus from the subject's skin to the distant wardrobe textures.
Subject & Identity Locking:
•Identity: Strictly preserve the exact face and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
•Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity "TrueLens" biological physics: visible micro-pores, a natural "satin-finish" night hydration glow, and fine vellus hair catching the light ONLY along the jawline. Her lower limbs show a healthy, smooth, and radiant surface texture.
•Silhouette Calibration (MAXIMUM UFF-FACTOR): Prioritize a dominant structural upper-torso volume and substantial, thick-toned anatomical depth in the lower extremities. Focus on an aggressive architectural tapering at the midsection to maximize the S-curve geometry and fit, symmetrical proportions.
Outfit & Textural Interaction:
•Clothing: A high-density matte-black strapless compressed textile bodice paired with a high-tension {argument name="skirt pattern" default="leopard-print"} technical mini-skirt featuring an adjustable cinch-drawstring on the lateral thigh.
•Tension: The technical fabrics exhibit extreme mechanical tension and a vacuum-tight fit, creating realistic textural expansion, skin-to-textile indentations, and "compression lines" where the materials contour to her fit silhouette.
•Detail: A small minimalist architectural ink marking is visible on the interior aspect of the right wrist.
Pose & Composition:
•Pose: A straight-on frontal mirror composition. She is standing with a bilateral hip-pop and an arched spine to maximize the visual impact of her fit, symmetrical 3D volume and lower-body prominence.
•Action: Her LEFT HAND holds a smartphone with a patterned protective casing, capturing the full-body "fit-check" reflection.
Environment & Social Realism:
•Lighting: Direct indoor illumination creating high-contrast specular highlights and deep, defining shadows that amplify her 3D form.
•Background (SHARP): A detailed domestic interior showing uncurated realism. A white faux-fur rug on light wood-grain flooring, a beige wardrobe with vertical structural lines, and a minimalist white wall are all perfectly IN FOCUS. No artificial blur.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the deep shadows (ISO 800 style), 8k resolution. Everything is sharp, detailed, and physically real.
```
